add_namespace = annex

#####################################
############  AFRICA   #############
#####################################


country_event = { #Fate of Morocco
	id = annex.4000
	title = annexation_461
	desc = annexation_461_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { 
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_morocco = yes 
					}
					add_core_of = MOR
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = MOR
					remove_claim_by = MOR
				}
			}
			1492 = { retain_state = yes } #Reguibat
			807 = { retain_state = yes } #Ibar Idjit
		}
	}

	#option = { #Establish West African Colonial State
	#	log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4000 option annex.establish_colony"
	#	name = annex.establish_colony
	#	trigger = {
	#		annexations_can_establish_african_colony = yes
	#	}
	#	annexations_release_BAF_colony = yes
	#}
	
	option = { #Release Morocco Whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4000 option annex.4000.a"
		name = annex.4000.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = MOR }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = MOR }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes


		if = { 
			limit = { has_custom_puppet_MOR = yes }
			setup_custom_puppet_MOR = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			MOR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MOR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			MOR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MOR_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4000 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_morocco = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4000 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_morocco = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4000 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
    }
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Reguibat
    id = annex.4001
    title = annexation_807
    desc = annexation_807_desc
    picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

    is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					461 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Casablanca
					920 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #El eglab
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				461 = { #Casablanca
					owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
				}
			}
			var:461.owner = { save_event_target_as = morocco }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				920 = { #El eglab
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						NOT = { tag = event_target:morocco }
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:920.owner = { save_event_target_as = carnegia }
		}
	}

    option = { #Give it to Morocco
        log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4001 option annex.4001.a"
        name = annex.4001.a
        trigger = {
			has_event_target = morocco
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:morocco }
				is_ally_with = event_target:carnegia
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = carnegia }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:morocco = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:morocco }
		807 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Reguibat
    }

    option = { #Give it to Carnegia
        log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4001 option annex.4001.b"
        name = annex.4001.b
        trigger = {
			has_event_target = carnegia
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:carnegia }
				is_ally_with = event_target:morocco
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = morocco }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:carnegia = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:carnegia }
		807 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Reguibat
    }

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4001 option name annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		807 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Reguibat
	}

	option = { #Annex
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4001 option name annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		807 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Reguibat
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4001 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Carnegia
	id = annex.4002
	title = annexation_920
	desc = annexation_920_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		807 = { retain_state = yes } #Reguibat
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						tag = MOR
						tag = ALG
						tag = MLI
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Carnegia Whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4002 option annex.4002.a"
		name = annex.4002.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 40
				BAF = {
					exists = yes
				}
				ROOT = {
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = BAF
						has_subject = BAF
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = WAF }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		
		hidden_effect = {
			WAF = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			}
			setup_custom_puppet_WAF = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4002 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_carnegia = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4002 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_carnegia = yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4002 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Algerian Concessions
	id = annex.4003
	title = annexation_838
	desc = annexation_838_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { 
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						539 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Algiers
						original_tag = FRA
						original_tag = ALG
						original_tag = BAF
						original_tag = INA
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					FRA = { 
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes 
					} 
				}
				FRA = { save_event_target_as = france }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					459 = { # Chlef
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:france }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:459.owner = { save_event_target_as = chlef }
			}
		}
    }

	option = { #Give to Chlef
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4003 option annex.4003.a"
		name = annex.4003.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = chlef
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:chlef }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:france
					is_ally_with = event_target:baf
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:chlef = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:chlef }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_algerian_concessions = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4003 option annex.4003.b"
		name = annex.4003.c
		trigger = {
            has_event_target = france
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:france }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:baf
					is_ally_with = event_target:chlef
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:france = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		838 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
		837 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
		1471 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
		1455 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # 
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4003 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		838 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #
		837 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } # 
		1471 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #
		1455 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4003 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		838 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #
		837 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } # 
		1471 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #|
		1455 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4003 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Rif
	id = annex.4004
	title = annexation_290
	desc = annexation_290_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					173 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes }
					1483 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes }
					118 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes }
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}

		# Target 173
		if = {
			limit = {
				173 = {
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
				}
			}
			var:173.owner = {
				save_event_target_as = rif_target_1
				add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
			}
		}

		# Target 1483
		if = {
			limit = {
				1483 = {
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						NOT = { tag = event_target:rif_target_1 }
					}
				}
			}
			var:1483.owner = {
				save_event_target_as = rif_target_2
				add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
			}
		}

		# Target 118
		if = {
			limit = {
				118 = {
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						NOT = { tag = event_target:rif_target_1 }
						NOT = { tag = event_target:rif_target_2 }
					}
				}
			}
			var:118.owner = {
				save_event_target_as = rif_target_3
				add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Give to target 1
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4004 option target 1"
		name = annex.4004.a

		trigger = {
			has_event_target = rif_target_1
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:rif_target_1 = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:rif_target_1 }
		290 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Rif
	}

	option = { # Give to target 2
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4004 option target 2"
		name = annex.4004.b

		trigger = {
			has_event_target = rif_target_2
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:rif_target_2 = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:rif_target_2 }
		290 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Rif
	}

	option = { # Give to target 3
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4004 option target 3"
		name = annex.4004.c

		trigger = {
			has_event_target = rif_target_3
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:rif_target_3 = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:rif_target_3 }
		290 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Rif
	}

	option = { # Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annexation_events.4004 option give_to_overlord"
		name = annexation_events.give_to_overlord

		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		290 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Rif
	}

	option = { # Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4004 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration

		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}

		290 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } # Rif
	}

	option = { # Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4004 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Algeria
	id = annex.4005
	title = annexation_460
	desc = annexation_460_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_algeria = yes }
					add_core_of = ALG
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = ALG
				}
				remove_claim_by = ALG
			}
		}
		837 = { retain_state = yes } #Oran
		838 = { retain_state = yes } #Algeirs
		1471 = { retain_state = yes} #Bougie
		1455 = { retain_state = yes} #Bone
		if = {
			limit = { 
				OR ={ 
					original_tag = FRA
					original_tag = ALG
					original_tag = BAF
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		annexations_create_baf_event_target = yes
		annexations_create_france_event_target = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Release Algeria Whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4005 option annex.4005.a"
		name = annex.4005.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ALG }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			ALG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ALG_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			ALG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ALG_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to baf
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4005 option annex.4005.b"
		name = annex.4005.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = baf
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:baf }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_algeria = yes }
			transfer_if_owned = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4005 option annex.4005.c"
		name = annex.4005.c
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = france
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_algeria = yes }
			transfer_if_owned = yes
		}
	}

	option = { # Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4005 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord

		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }

		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_algeria = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { # Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4005 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration

		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}

		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_algeria = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4005 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		
    }
	after = { 
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Tunisia
	id = annex.4006
	title = annexation_458
	desc = annexation_458_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_tunisia = yes }
					add_core_of = TUN
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = TUN
				}
				remove_claim_by = TUN
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = BAF 
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Batna owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				1470 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						tag = BAF
					}
				}
			}
			var:1470.owner = { save_event_target_as = batna }
		}
		# Optional retention (adjust if needed)
		1472 = { retain_state = yes }
		annexations_create_baf_event_target = yes
	}

	option = { # Release Tunisia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4006 option annex.4006.a"
		name = annex.4006.a

		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0 }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = TUN }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			TUN = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = TUN_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Give to Batna
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4006 option annex.4006.b"
		name = annex.4006.b

		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = batna
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 70
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:batna
					has_subject = event_target:batna
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:batna = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:batna }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_tunisia = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { # Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4006 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord

		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }

country_event = { #Fate of Zanzibar
	id = annex.4026
	title = annexation_1306
	desc = annexation_1306_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = OMA
					tag = TGY
					tag = DEA
					1558 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Tanganyika
					294 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Masqat
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Tanganyika
		if = {
			limit = {
				1558 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1558.owner = { save_event_target_as = tanganyika }
		}
		
		# Masqat
		if = {
			limit = {
				294 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						tag = OMA
					}
				}
			}
			var:294.owner = { save_event_target_as = masqat }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Zanzibar
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4026 option annex.4026.a"
		name = annex.4026.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ZAN }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			ZAN = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ZAN_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			ZAN = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ZAN_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to Tanganyika
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4026 option annex.4026.b"
		name = annex.4026.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = tanganyika
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				is_ally_with = event_target:tanganyika
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:masqat }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:tanganyika }
				is_ally_with = event_target:masqat
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:tanganyika }
        1306 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }
	}

	option = { #Give to Masqat
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4026 option annex.4026.c"
		name = annex.4026.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = masqat
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 70
				is_ally_with = event_target:masqat
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:tanganyika }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:masqat }
				is_ally_with = event_target:tanganyika
			}

country_event = { #Fate of Trarza
	id = annex.4028
	title = annexation_808
	desc = annexation_808_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		every_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_trarza = no }
				remove_core_of = MRT
			}
			else = {
				add_core_of = MRT
			}
			remove_claim_by = MRT
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = BAF
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		931 = { retain_state = yes } #Dakhla
		786 = { retain_state = yes } #Oualata
		1495 = { retain_state = yes } #Brakna
		926 = { retain_state = yes } #Selibaby

			}
        }

			base = 100

				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes

		}

			}

						}

			}

country_event = { #Fate of Beni Abbas / Mali / Timbuktu
	id = annex.4032
	title = annexation_861
	desc = annexation_861_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		786 = { retain_state = yes } #Oualata
		1517 = { retain_state = yes } #Gourma-Rharous
		every_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_mali = no }
				remove_core_of = MLI
			}
			else = {
				add_core_of = MLI
			}
			remove_claim_by = MLI
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Beni Abbas whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4032 option annex.4032.a"
		name = annex.4032.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = MLI }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			MLI = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = MLI_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			MLI = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = MLI_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to British African Authority
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4032 option annex.4032.b"
		name = annex.4032.b
		trigger = {
			BAF = {
				exists = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				OR = {
					has_subject = BAF
					is_ally_with = BAF
				}
			}
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { 
				is_mali = yes 
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4032 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = { 
				is_mali = yes 
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4032 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { 
				is_mali = yes 
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4032 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

country_event = { #Fate of Wattaria
	id = annex.4037
	title = annexation_804
	desc = annexation_804_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		925 = { retain_state = yes } #Bamako
	}
	
	option = { #Release Wattaria whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4037 option annex.4037.a"
		name = annex.4037.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				ROOT = {
					OR = {
						has_subject = BAF
						is_ally_with = BAF
					}
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = WAT }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			WAT = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = WAT_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			WAT = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = WAT_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to British African Authority
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4037 option annex.4037.b"
		name = annex.4037.b
		trigger = {
			BAF = {
				exists = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				OR = {
					has_subject = BAF
					is_ally_with = BAF
				}
			}
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = BAF }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { 
				is_wattaria = yes 
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4037 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = { 
				is_wattaria = yes 
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4037 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { 
				is_wattaria = yes 
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4037 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Nigeria
	id = annex.4043
	title = annexation_558
	desc = annexation_558_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		every_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_nigeria = no }
				remove_core_of = NIG
			}
			else = {
				add_core_of = NIG
			}
			remove_claim_by = NIG
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = BAF
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Nigeria whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4043 option annex.4043.a"
		name = annex.4043.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 30
				BAF = {
					exists = yes
				}
				ROOT = {
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = BAF
						has_subject = BAF
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = NIG }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			NIG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = NIG_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			NIG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = NIG_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Divide Nigeria
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4043 option annex.4043.a"
		name = annex.4043.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = NIG
			}
			remove_core_of = NIG
		}
		558 = { add_core_of = SOK }
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SOK }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			SOK = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = SOK_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			SOK = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = SOK_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to British African Authority
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4043 option annex.4043.b"
		name = annex.4043.c
		trigger = {
			BAF = {
				exists = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = BAF
					has_subject = BAF
				}
			}
        }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = BAF }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_nigeria = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4043 option annex.give_to_overlord"

		trigger = {

			limit = {

				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4043 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_owned_state = {

				factor = 0

				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:zamfara = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		}

country_event = { #Fate of Tripolitania
	id = annex.4060
	title = annexation_448
	desc = annexation_448_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = CYR
					AND = {
						tag = EGY
						owns_state = 450
					}
					450 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Benghazi
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Benghazi
		if = {
			limit = {
				450 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:450.owner = { save_event_target_as = benghazi }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Libya
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4060 option annex.4060.a"
		name = annex.4060.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			LBA = { exists = no }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				ROOT = { 
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = event_target:benghazi 
						has_subject = event_target:benghazi
					}
				}
			}
		}
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_tripolitania = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			add_core_of = LBA
		}
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = LBA }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			LBA = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = LBA_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			LBA = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = LBA_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to Benghazi
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4060 option annex.4060.b"
		name = annex.4060.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = benghazi
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:benghazi }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:benghazi = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:benghazi }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_tripolitania = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4060 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_tripolitania = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4060 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_tripolitania = yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4060 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

				}
			}

country_event = { #Fate of Bechuanaland
	id = annex.4088
	title = annexation_1318
	desc = annexation_1318_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		1571 = { retain_state = yes } #Stellaland
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					1564 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Namaland
					1569 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Central Kaap
					original_tag = PRE
					original_tag = SDW
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Namaland owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				1564 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1564.owner = { save_event_target_as = namaland }
		}
		
		# Central Kaap owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				1569 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1569.owner = { save_event_target_as = centralkaap }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Bechuanaland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4088 option annex.4088.a"
		name = annex.4088.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = SDW
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
			modifier = {
				factor = 30
				OR = {
					original_tag = GER
					original_tag = PRE
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				OR = {
					original_tag = SWA
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = BOT }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					NOT = {
						tag = SWA
					}
				}
			}
			BOT = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = BOT_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					tag = SWA
				}
			}
			BOT = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = BOT_SWA_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			BOT = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = BOT_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Namaland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4088 option annex.4088.b"
		name = annex.4088.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = namaland
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 80
				SDW = {
					exists = yes
				}
				ROOT = {
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = SDW
						has_subject = SDW
					}
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:namaland }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_bechuanaland = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Central Kaap
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4088 option annex.4088.c"
		name = annex.4088.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = centralkaap
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				PRE = {
					exists = yes
				}
				ROOT = {
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = PRE
						has_subject = PRE
					}
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:centralkaap }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_bechuanaland = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4088 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_bechuanaland = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4088 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_bechuanaland = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4088 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	id = annex.4089
	title = annexation_1571
	desc = annexation_1571_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					275 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Transvaal
					858 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Bloemfontain
					1318 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Bechuanaland
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Transvaal owner
		if = {
			limit = {
				275 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:275.owner = { save_event_target_as = transvaal }

		if = {

					}

		if = {

						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}

		}

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:bloemfontain }
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:bechuanaland }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:transvaal
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:transvaal }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:bloemfontain
					is_ally_with = event_target:bechuanaland
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:transvaal }
        1571 = {
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Bloemfontain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4089 option annex.4089.b"
		name = annex.4089.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}

				factor = 90
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:transvaal }
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:bechuanaland }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:bloemfontain
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:bloemfontain }
				OR = {

					is_ally_with = event_target:bechuanaland

				is_ally_with = event_target:bechuanaland

			modifier = {

				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:bechuanaland }

	id = annex.4093
	title = annexation_1326
	desc = annexation_1326_desc

	immediate = {
		770 = { retain_state = yes } #Nyasa
		879 = { retain_state = yes } #Gazaland
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = PEF
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Mozambique
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4093 option annex.4093.a"
		name = annex.4093.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = PEF
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = MZB }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			MZB = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MZB_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			MZB = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MZB_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to the Portuguese Exiles
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4093 option annex.4093.b"
		name = annex.4093.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			PEF = {
				exists = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = PEF
				}
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = PEF
					has_subject = PEF
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PEF }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_mozambique = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4093 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_mozambique = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4093 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = MZB
					original_tag = ZIM
					1326 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Sofala
					1324 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Matabeleland
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		# Sofala owner
		if = {

		if = {
			limit = {
				1324 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1324.owner = { save_event_target_as = matabeleland }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Capeloland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4101 option annex.4101.a"
		name = annex.4101.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = MZB
					original_tag = ZIM
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = CAP }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			CAP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = CAP_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			CAP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = CAP_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Sofala
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4101 option annex.4101.b"
		name = annex.4101.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = sofala
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:matabeleland }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:sofala
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:sofala }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:matabeleland
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:sofala }
        545 = {
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Matabeleland

				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ZIM_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			ZIM = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ZIM_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Barotseland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4102 option annex.4102.b"
		name = annex.4102.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = barotseland
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:lusaka }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:barotseland
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:barotseland }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:lusaka
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:barotseland }
        1324 = {
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Lusaka
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4102 option annex.4102.b"
		name = annex.4102.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = lusaka
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:barotseland }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:lusaka
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:lusaka }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:barotseland
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:lusaka }
        1324 = {
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4102 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
        1324 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
				}
				transfer_state_and_notify = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4102 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}

			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel

		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				864 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:864.owner = { save_event_target_as = garanganza }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				873 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}

		}

			limit = {
				868 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:868.owner = { save_event_target_as = barotseland }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Garanganza
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4107 option annex.4107.a"
		name = annex.4107.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = garanganza
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:chokweland }
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:barotseland }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:garanganza
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:garanganza }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:chokweland
					is_ally_with = event_target:barotseland
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:garanganza }
        1323 = {
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	id = annex.4108
	title = annexation_864
	desc = annexation_864_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_country_pink_map = yes
					1547 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Kasai
					873 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Chokweland
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		
		# Kasai
		if = {
			limit = {
				1547 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1547.owner = { save_event_target_as = kasai }
		}
		
		# Chokweland
		if = {
			limit = {
				873 = {
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						tag = PEF
					}
				}
			}
			var:873.owner = { save_event_target_as = chokweland }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Garanganza
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4108 option annex.4108.a"
		name = annex.4108.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = PEF
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = GAR }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			GAR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = GAR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			GAR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = GAR_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Kasai
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4108 option annex.4108.b"
		name = annex.4108.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = kasai
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				AND = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:chokweland }
				}
				is_ally_with = event_target:kasai
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:kasai }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:chokweland
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:kasai }
        every_owned_state = {

				is_garanganza = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes

			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

				is_ally_with = event_target:chokweland
			}

				factor = 0

				}

	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Africa

				remove_core_of = SEN
				remove_claim_by = SEN
				#
				remove_core_of = BAF
				remove_claim_by = BAF
			}
			272 = {
				add_core_of = SEN
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						tag = BAF

						tag = FUL
						808 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Trarza
						298 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Futa Djallon
					}

			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					808 = {
						owner = {
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							tag = MRT
						}
					}
				}
				var:808.owner = { save_event_target_as = trarza }
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Release Senegal
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4122 option annex.4122.a"
		name = annex.4122.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			SEN = { exists = no }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 20
				ROOT = {
					tag = FRA
					tag = GER
					tag = ENG
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SEN }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			SEN = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SEN_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			SEN = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SEN_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Trarza
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.4122 option annex.4122.b"
		name = annex.4122.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = trarza
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 65
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:trarza }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:futa_djallon
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:trarza = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:trarza }
        272 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }
	}
	
	option = { #Release a West African Colonial Administration

			is_subject = no

			}
		}
